结论Conclusion

建模天才：Modeling Genius:

探索生生不息的智慧（GQ）的基础Exploring the Foundations of Generative Intelligence (GQ)

The core of Neuro-Linguistic Programming (NLP) since its foundation has been the modeling of human excellence. At the heart of modeling lies a deceptively simple yet profoundly generative question originally articulated by Gregory Bateson: What is the difference that makes the difference? This question continues to guide me not only in my own application of NLP but also in a broader exploration into the nature of human genius, creativity, and transformation.

神经-语言程序学（NLP）自创立以来，其核心便是人类卓越表现的建模（modeling）。建模的核心，是一个看似简单却深具生成性的问题，最初由格里高里·贝特森提出：造成差异的差异是什么？（What is the difference that makes the difference?）这个问题持续引导着我，不仅在我的 NLP 应用中，也在对人类天才、创造力与转化之本质的更广探索之中。

From the beginning, NLP set out not merely to describe excellence, but to understand its underlying subjective structure — those deeper patterns of thinking, perceiving and inner representation that enable certain individuals to produce extraordinary results. The aim was never imitation for its own sake. Rather, it was to discover how excellence is produced, so that it might be cultivated, extended, and ultimately recreated within diverse contexts in new and evolving forms.

从一开始，NLP 的志向便不止于描述卓越，更在于理解其底层的主观结构——那些使特定个体得以产生非凡结果的、更深层的思考、感知与内在表征模式。其目标从来不是为模仿而模仿。相反，是要发现卓越是如何产生的，好让它能被培育、延展，并最终以崭新而演化的形式，在多元脉络中重新被创造。

卓越的深层结构The Deeper Structure of Excellence

Traditional approaches to learning often emphasize the replication of behavior — observing what successful individuals do and attempting to reproduce those actions. While this can yield useful results, it rarely captures the essence of true mastery. What NLP modeling revealed early on is that behavior is only the surface expression of deeper internal processes.

传统的学习取径，常强调行为的复制——观察成功个体做什么，并试图复制那些行动。这虽能带来有用结果，却很少捕捉到真正精通的精髓。NLP 建模早年便揭示：行为只是更深层内在过程的表层表达。

Exceptional individuals do not simply do different things; they perceive differently, organize experience differently, and relate to systems differently. Their actions emerge from a rich interplay of beliefs, values, sensory awareness, inner representations and systemic understanding.

卓越的个体并非只是做不同的事；他们感知不同、组织经验不同、与系统关联不同。他们的行动，源于信念、价值、感官觉察、内在表征与系统理解之间丰富的互动。

Thus, the focus of NLP modeling involves the shift from what people do to how they do it:

因此，NLP 建模的焦点，涉及从人们做什么到他们如何做的转移：

  • How they filter and interpret reality
  • How they organize their internal representations
  • How they make decisions and determine appropriate actions
  • How they interact with the larger systems of which they are a part
  • 他们如何过滤与诠释现实
  • 他们如何组织自己的内在表征
  • 他们如何做出决定、并判定恰当的行动
  • 他们如何与自己所身处之更大系统互动

This is what I have been calling a "strategy". It is at this level of patterning that true excellence resides and can be most effectively transferred to others.

这正是我所谓的"策略"（strategy）。正是在这一模式化的层次上，真正的卓越栖身其中，也最能有效地传递给他人。

我在人类发展领域与天才的相遇My Encounters with Genius in the Area of Human Development

On my own journey, I have had the extraordinary privilege of personally encountering Milton Erickson, Gregory Bateson, Moshe

在我自身的旅程中，我有幸亲自遇见米尔顿·艾瑞克森、格里高里·贝特森、摩西·费登奎斯

Feldenkrais, and Gabrielle Roth – individuals whose genius has shaped entire fields of human understanding and development. As I have explored in this book, each of these individuals expressed their genius through a unique modality:

与加布里埃尔·罗斯——这些天才塑造了人类理解与发展的整个领域。正如我在本书中所探究的，这些个体各自通过一种独特的模态表达其天才：

  • Erickson through language, metaphor, and unconscious communication
  • Bateson through systemic thinking, integrating multiple perspectives and distinguishing different levels of process
  • Feldenkrais through movement, somatic learning and embodied awareness
  • Roth through dance, rhythm and group interaction
  • 艾瑞克森 通过语言、隐喻与无意识沟通
  • 贝特森 通过系统思考、整合多重视角，以及区分过程的不同层次
  • 费登奎斯 通过运动、体感学习与具身觉察
  • 罗斯 通过舞蹈、节奏与群体互动

At first glance, their approaches appear significantly different. One works with words, another with ideas, another with the body, and the other with expressive action and rhythm. Yet, as I became more familiar with each of their methods, a key realization emerged: their effectiveness did not come from rigid techniques or fixed methods. In fact, what made them extraordinary was precisely their freedom from technique.

乍看之下，他们的方法似乎大不相同。一个用词语工作，一个用观念，一个用身体，另一个用表达性行动与节奏。然而，当我愈发熟悉他们各自的方法，一个关键的认识浮现：他们的效力，并非来自僵化的技术或固定的方法。事实上，使他们非凡的，恰恰是他们从技术中获得的自由。

人类发展中天才的共同基础The Common Ground of Genius in Human Development

Despite their differences in modality, these individuals shared a set of underlying principles — what might be called the generative structure of genius. These principles are not techniques in themselves, but orientations toward experience that enable the emergence of new possibilities.

尽管模态各异，这些个体共享一组底层原则——或可称为天才的生成性结构。这些原则本身并非技术，而是朝向经验的取向，使新可能性的涌现成为可能。

1. Respect for Unconscious and Embodied Intelligence

1. 尊重无意识与具身的智能

Each of these geniuses recognized that intelligence is not confined to conscious thought — that there is a more inclusive intelligence beyond the cognitive mind, the unconscious mind, and the broader ecosystem all possess forms of wisdom that can guide change in a naturally ecological manner. Milton Erickson, for example, trusted the unconscious mind to generate solutions uniquely suited to each individual. Moshe Feldenkrais worked with subtle movements to awaken the body's innate capacity for learning and adaptation. Gabrielle Roth accessed transformation through rhythm and embodied expression. In each case, change was not imposed from the outside but evoked from within.

这些天才中的每一个，都认识到智能并不局限于有意识的思考——在认知心智、无意识心智与更广的生态系统之外，还存在着更包容的智能，它们都拥有能以自然符合生态的方式引导改变的智慧。例如，米尔顿·艾瑞克森信任无意识心智，去生成独特契合每个人的解法。摩西·费登奎斯运用精微的运动，唤醒身体学习与适应的天生能力。加布里埃尔·罗斯通过节奏与具身表达取用转化。在每一例中，改变都不是从外部强加，而是从内部唤起。

2. Trust in Self-Organizing Processes

2. 信任自组织的过程

A second shared principle is a deep trust in the self-organizing nature of living systems. Rather than attempting to control outcomes directly, these individuals created conditions in which change could naturally emerge. Gregory Bateson emphasized the importance of understanding the patterns that connect systems. He recognized that systems — whether individuals, families, or ecosystems — tend toward naturally self-organizing optimization when properly supported. This perspective shifts the role of the practitioner from "fixer" to "facilitator." The goal is not to force change, but to create the context in which change becomes possible — even inevitable.

第二项共享原则，是对生命系统自组织本性的深切信任。这些个体不是试图直接控制结果，而是创造改变能自然涌现的条件。格里高里·贝特森强调理解连接系统的模式的重要性。他认识到：系统——无论是个体、家庭还是生态系统——在得到恰当地支持时，会趋向自然的自组织优化。这一视角，将从业者的角色从"修理者"转换为"促进者"。目标不是强行改变，而是创造改变得以可能——甚至必然——发生的脉络。

3. Sensitivity to Context and Relationship

3. 对脉络与关系的敏感

Another hallmark of genius with respect to human development is an acute sensitivity to context. What works in one situation may not work in another. Rather than applying standardized techniques, these individuals responded dynamically to the unique features of each situation. Erickson, for example, would tailor his communication to the specific language patterns, beliefs and experiences of each client. Feldenkrais adapted movements based on subtle feedback from the body. Roth allowed the rhythm of the moment to guide the process. In all cases, effectiveness arose from a deep attunement to relationship — between self and other, between parts of a system, and between individuals and their environment.

人类发展中天才的另一标志，是对脉络的敏锐敏感。在一种情境中有效的，在另一种情境中未必有效。这些个体不是套用标准化的技术，而是对每一情境的独特特征做出动态回应。例如，艾瑞克森会根据每位客户特定的语言模式、信念与经验，量身定制他的沟通。费登奎斯依据身体细微的反馈调整运动。罗斯让当下时刻的节奏来引导过程。在所有例子中，效力都源于对关系——自我与他人之间、系统各部分之间、个体与其环境之间——的深度调频。

4. Orientation Toward Wholeness

4. 朝向完整性的取向

Perhaps the most profound commonality between these geniuses is an orientation toward wholeness rather than fragmentation. Rather than isolating problems or breaking experience into disconnected parts, each of them worked to create greater integration. They recognized that symptoms often arise from disconnection — between mind and body, between individual and environment, between intention and action. Their work aimed to restore coherence, alignment, and flow. This orientation transforms the very nature of change. Instead of "fixing what is broken," the focus becomes "reconnecting what has been separated."

或许这些天才之间最深刻的共通处，是一种朝向完整性（wholeness）而非碎裂（fragmentation）的取向。他们不是隔离问题、或将经验拆成互不相干的部分，而是每个人都致力于创造更大的整合。他们认识到，症状常常源于失联——心智与身体之间、个体与环境之间、意图与行动之间。他们的工作旨在恢复连贯、对齐与流动。这一取向，转化了改变的本质。焦点从"修理破损之物"，变成了"重新联结被分离之物"。

建模生成性Modeling Generativity

The essence of "genius" is generativity — the capacity to create something new. Like everything we humans do, this is a process that has a structure. One of the paradoxes of creativity and genius is that they require structure and discipline at a certain level. As Gabrielle Roth used to ask, "Do you have the discipline to be a free spirit?"

"天才"的本质，是生成性（generativity）——创造崭新事物的能力。如同我们人类所做的一切，这是一个有其结构的过程。创造力与天才的悖论之一，是它们在某一层次上需要结构与纪律。正如加布里埃尔·罗斯曾经问的："你是否拥有成为自由灵魂的纪律？"

Roth would also counsel those students who said they wanted to be like her, "If you want to be like me then be more like you." As I wrote in Strategies of Genius Volume 3, modeling genius is not about "following in the footsteps" of the individuals we are studying but rather about "seeking what they sought." This distinction marks the movement from replication to generativity. To follow in someone's footsteps is to walk the same path they walked. To seek what they sought is to pursue the same orientation toward discovery, even if it leads to entirely new destinations.

罗斯也会劝导那些说想变得像她的学生："如果你想变得像我，那就变得更像你自己。"正如我在《天才的策略 第三卷》（Strategies of Genius Volume 3）中所写，建模天才，不是"踩着我们所研究之个体的足迹"，而是"追寻他们所追寻的"。这一区分，标志了从复制到生成性的移动。踩着某人的足迹，是走他们走过的同一条路。追寻他们所追寻的，是追求同一种朝向发现的取向，即便它通向全然崭新的目的地。

生生不息的智慧的关键能力Key Capacities of Generative Intelligence

Generative Intelligence (or GQ) can be understood as the capacity to produce new patterns, new meanings, and new possibilities. It is not bound by past solutions but is continually evolving in response to new contexts. With respect to human development, this form of intelligence involves several key capacities:

生生不息的智慧（或称 GQ），可被理解为产生新模式、新意义与新可能性的能力。它不被过去的解法所束缚，而是持续回应新脉络而演化。就人类发展而言，这种智能涉及若干关键能力：

  • Perceptual flexibility: The ability to see situations from multiple perspectives
  • Systemic awareness: Understanding how elements interact within a larger whole
  • Creative responsiveness: The capacity to adapt and innovate in real time
  • 感知灵活性： 从多重视角看情境的能力
  • 系统性觉察： 理解元素如何在更大整体中互动
  • 创造性回应： 实时适应与创新的能力
  • Alignment with deeper purpose: Acting in accordance with values and intentions that transcend immediate outcomes
  • 与更深目标的 alignment： 依循超越当下结果的价值与意图而行动

Generative intelligence is not something one "has" but something one cultivates. It emerges through practice, mindful reflection on experience and engagement with complexity.

生生不息的智慧不是人"拥有"的东西，而是人培育的东西。它透过实践、对经验的 mindful 反思，以及与复杂性的 engagement 而涌现。

成为卓越的源头Becoming a Source of Excellence

In this context, the role of the modeler becomes that of an explorer and translator of human potential. Rather than extracting formulas, the modeler seeks to uncover the principles that give rise to excellence. The goal is not to create clones of existing geniuses, but to enable others to access their own unique expressions of excellence.

在此脉络下，建模者的角色，成了人类潜能的探索者与转译者。建模者寻求揭示的，不是提取公式，而是催生卓越的原则。目标不是创造既有天才的克隆，而是让他人能够取用他们自身独特的卓越表达。

Ultimately, the focus of modeling genius is not on the past, but on the future. It is not about preserving what has already been done, but about expanding what is possible. To model genius is to ask:

最终，建模天才的焦点不在过去，而在未来。它不是保存已经做过的事，而是拓展可能之事。建模天才，便是去问：

  • What principles allow creativity and excellence to emerge?
  • How can these principles be cultivated within ourselves and others?
  • How can we apply them in new domains and contexts?
  • 什么原则让创造力与卓越得以涌现？
  • 这些原则如何能于我们自身与他人之中被培育？
  • 我们如何能将它们应用于崭新的领域与脉络？

In this sense, modeling itself becomes a generative act — a way of participating in the ongoing evolution of human capability.

在此意义上，建模本身成为一种生成性的行动——一种参与人类能力持续演化的方式。

追寻他们所追寻的Seeking What They Sought

The true legacy of the great innovators and visionaries is not the specific methods they used, but the spirit of inquiry and discovery they embodied.

伟大的创新者与远见者的真正遗产，不是他们所用的特定方法，而是他们所具身化的探究与发现之精神。

Milton Erickson did not leave behind a fixed set of techniques to be replicated. Gregory Bateson did not offer a single model of systems thinking to be applied universally. Moshe Feldenkrais and Gabrielle Roth did not prescribe particular movements in rigid terms. What they offered instead was an invitation — an invitation to explore, to perceive more deeply, to trust the intelligence of life, and to participate in the unfolding of new possibilities.

米尔顿·艾瑞克森并未留下一套待复制的固定技术。格里高里·贝特森未提供单一放之四海皆准的系统思考模型。摩西·费登奎斯与加布里埃尔·罗斯，也未以僵化的术语规定特定的运动。他们所提供的，是一个邀请——一个去探索、去更深地感知、去信任生命之智能、并参与新可能性之展开的邀请。

In this sense, modeling genius becomes a pathway not to imitation, but to transformation. It is a journey from asking, "How can I do what they did?" to asking, "How can I become a source of what is yet to be created?"

在此意义上，建模天才成为一条通往转化、而非模仿的路径。这是一场从"我如何能做到他们所做的？"到"我如何能成为那尚未被创造之物的源头？"的旅程。

And in that shift, we move from reproducing excellence to generating it.

而在那转换之中，我们从复制卓越，移动到生成卓越。

附录 A："机器人迪尔茨"Appendix A: "Robot Dilts"

生生不息的智慧遇见人工智能Generative Intelligence Meets Artificial Intelligence

"Robot Dilts" is an AI agent (actually a collection of AI agents) inspired by the principles, models and methodologies I have developed over decades of work in NLP, Success Factor Modeling, coaching, leadership, learning, creativity and human development. Much more than a source of information, Robot Dilts is designed to function as a collaborative thinking partner — helping users clarify goals, discover new perspectives, access their resources, learn new skills, solve problems and create meaningful and sustainable change.

"机器人迪尔茨"（Robot Dilts）是一个 AI 智能体（实际上是一群 AI 智能体），其灵感来自我数十年来在 NLP、成功要素建模（Success Factor Modeling）、教练、领导力、学习、创造力与人类发展中所发展的原则、模型与方法论。机器人迪尔茨远不止是一个信息来源，它被设计为一个协作式的思考伙伴——帮助使用者厘清目标、发现新视角、取用其资源、学习新技能、解决问题，并创造有意义且可持续的改变。

At its core, Robot Dilts embodies the fundamental principles of Generative Intelligence: the ability to perceive and connect patterns, perspectives, and possibilities in ways that produce new choices and innovative solutions. Rather than simply providing answers, Robot Dilts helps people ask better questions, identify deeper purposes, align their values, strengthen their capabilities, and translate insights into effective action.

在其核心，机器人迪尔茨具身了生生不息的智慧的基本原则：以产生新选择与革新性解法的方式，去感知并连接模式、视角与可能性。机器人迪尔茨不只是提供答案，而是帮助人们提出更好的问题、辨识更深的目标、对齐其价值、强化其能力，并将洞见转化为有效的行动。

Artificial Intelligence can support Generative Intelligence by serving as a catalyst for reflection, modeling, learning, and synergy. When combined with Generative Intelligence, with Generative Intelligence, AI can help people recognize patterns in their thinking, explore alternative viewpoints, simulate possibilities and organize complex information into coherent strategies. When integrated with human wisdom, intuition, and purpose, AI becomes more than an intimidating technology — it becomes a partner in expanding awareness and generating new pathways for growth.

人工智能能通过作为反思、建模、学习与协同的催化剂，来支撑生生不息的智慧。当与生生不息的智慧结合，AI 能帮助人们辨识其思维中的模式、探索替代性的观点、模拟可能性，并将复杂信息组织为连贯的策略。当与人类智慧、直觉与目标整合，AI 不再只是一种令人生畏的技术——它成了拓展觉察、并生成崭新成长路径的伙伴。

Robot Dilts represents this partnership between human and artificial intelligence. Its purpose is not to replace human creativity and judgment, but to amplify them — helping individuals, teams, and organizations move beyond problem solving toward the creation of new futures that are more resourceful, meaningful, and generative.

机器人迪尔茨代表了人类智能与人工智能之间的这一伙伴关系。它的目的不是取代人类的创造力与判断，而是放大它们——帮助个体、团队与组织，超越问题解决，迈向创造更具资源、更有意义、更生成性的新未来。

Robot Dilts is not a generic "chatbot" like ChatGPT, Gemini, Claude, Grok, etc., which only have access (or legally should only have access) to what is in the public domain on the Internet (more and more of which is "AI slop" being generated by Artificial Intelligence rather than human ingenuity and creativity). Robot Dilts has a "Targeted Knowledge Base" composed of specific files containing my latest developments and unique intellectual property that is used to train the agent.

机器人迪尔茨并非像 ChatGPT、Gemini、Claude、Grok 等那样的通用"聊天机器人"，后者只（或依法只应）能取用互联网上公共领域的内容（其中越来越多的"AI 糟粕"〔AI slop〕正由人工智能而非人类的巧思与创造力所生成）。机器人迪尔茨拥有一个"定向知识库"（Targeted Knowledge Base），由包含我最新发展与独特知识产权的特定文件组成，用以训练该智能体。

Robot Dilts agents are also guided by customized "base prompts," which are the specific instructions that I give that define the agent's purpose, identity (role and mission) values and beliefs, capabilities, behavior and the boundaries ("guardrails") that determine the agent's personality and scope of interaction. Robot Dilts agents have also been trained in my unique communication style, tone, and vocabulary. These agents also have access to

机器人迪尔茨的智能体，还受定制"基础提示"（base prompts）的引导——那是我给出的特定指令，定义了智能体的目的、身份（角色与使命）、价值与信念、能力、行为，以及决定其个性与互动范围的边界（"护栏"〔guardrails〕）。机器人迪尔茨的智能体，也受训于我独特的沟通风格、语气与词汇。这些智能体还能取用

handpicked Media Resources — specific proprietary images, videos or audio files agents will use in their interactions with users.

精挑细选的媒体资源——智能体在与用户互动时会使用的特定专有图像、视频或音频文件。

机器人迪尔茨 AI 智能体的类型Types of Robot Dilts AI Agents

  • Tool – Executes a specific function, such as analyzing user's statements for particular distinctions and patterns
  • Coach – Guides users through the steps of a coaching process
  • Mentor – Responds to user's questions regarding a particular content area offering suggestions or advice
  • Modeler – Identifies and organizes key patterns in inputs provided by users, and creates applications if requested
  • Teacher – Helps users develop a specific skill through specific practices
  • Assistant – Supports users to understand and apply a particular model or skill set
  • Assessor – Assesses users with respect to particular patterns and offers suggestions for further development
  • Companion – Accompanies users on a learning journey (such as reading a book or attending a program) answering questions and offering guidance
  • 工具（Tool） – 执行特定功能，例如分析用户的陈述以辨识特定的区分与模式
  • 教练（Coach） – 引导用户走过某一教练过程的步骤
  • 导师（Mentor） – 回应用户关于某一内容领域的问题，提供建议或忠告
  • 建模者（Modeler） – 辨识并组织用户输入中的关键模式，并按要求创建应用
  • 教师（Teacher） – 通过特定练习帮助用户发展特定技能
  • 助手（Assistant） – 支持用户理解并应用某一模型或技能组
  • 评估者（Assessor） – 就特定模式评估用户，并提供进一步发展的建议
  • 同伴（Companion） – 陪伴用户走过一段学习旅程（如读一本书或参加一个项目），回答问题并提供引导

一些与天才的策略相关的机器人迪尔茨智能体Some Robot Dilts Agents Related Strategies of Genius

There are several Robot Dilts agents that are directly relevant to learning, practicing and applying Strategies of Genius.

有数个机器人迪尔茨智能体，与学习、实践并应用天才的策略直接相关。

机器人迪尔茨 天才的策略精灵（Strategies of Genius Genie）Robot Dilts Strategies of Genius Genie

An AI coach that applies Strategies of Genius to help people generate new ideas, solve problems, perform more effectively and make better decisions. It does this by:

一个运用天才的策略来帮助人们生成新点子、解决问题、更有效率地表现，并做出更好决定的 AI 教练。它这样做是通过：

  • Explaining and breaking down the unique strategies, models, and creative processes of the geniuses profiled in Robert Dilts' Strategies of Genius Volumes I-IV in clear, practical terms.
  • Guiding users step-by-step in applying these strategies to their own challenges, goals, or creative projects.
  • Helping users reflect, ask clarifying questions, and suggest actionable next steps tailored to their needs.
  • 以清晰、实用的术语，解释并拆解罗伯特·迪尔茨《天才的策略 第一至四卷》中所刻画之天才的独特策略、模型与创造过程。
  • 逐步引导用户，将这些策略应用于他们自身的挑战、目标或创意项目。
  • 帮助用户反思、提出澄清性的问题，并建议契合其需求的、可行动的下一步。

机器人迪尔茨 AI 生生不息的智慧评估者Robot Dilts AI Generative Intelligence Assessor

An AI agent that assesses a user's level of generative intelligence (GQ) — the ability to create new patterns, meanings, and possibilities in response to changing contexts and emerging realities. It does this by:

一个评估用户生生不息的智慧（GQ）水平——即在回应变化的脉络与涌现的现实时，创造新模式、新意义与新可能性的能力——的 AI 智能体。它这样做是通过：

  • Assessing users' strengths and growth areas across 10 dimensions of generative intelligence
  • Supporting reflection, creativity, and systemic awareness
  • 在生生不息的智慧的 10 个维度上，评估用户的优势与成长领域
  • 支撑反思、创造力与系统性觉察
  • Offering practical tools, feedback, and developmental practices tailored to users' contexts
  • Helping users become more adaptive, resourceful, and aligned with their deeper purpose — so they can have a greater positive impact in their work and life
  • 提供契合用户脉络的实用工具、反馈与发展性练习
  • 帮助用户变得更具适应性、更有资源、并与他们更深的目标对齐——好让他们能在工作与生活中产生更大的正面影响

机器人迪尔茨 AI 建模者Robot Dilts AI Modeler

An AI agent who applies the principles, methodologies and distinctions of NeuroLinguistic Programming and Success Factor Modeling to help to create models of human excellence. It does this by:

一个运用神经-语言程序学与成功要素建模的原则、方法论与区分，来帮助创建人类卓越模型的 AI 智能体。它这样做是通过：

  • Helping users clarify their goals and outcomes
  • Eliciting and mapping their thinking and behavioral strategies
  • Analyzing and organizing patterns using NLP frameworks
  • Suggesting practical steps and scripts for personal change or skill-building
  • Supporting users in applying modeling to real-life challenges — whether that's creativity, learning, leadership, emotional intelligence, or technical problem-solving
  • 帮助用户厘清他们的目标与结果
  • 引出并绘制他们的思考与行为策略
  • 运用 NLP 框架分析与组织模式
  • 建议用于个人改变或技能建立的实用步骤与脚本
  • 支撑用户将建模应用于现实挑战——无论是创造力、学习、领导力、情绪智能，还是技术性的问题求解

Users can ask it to help them break down their own processes, model the strategies of others, create step-by-step practices, or explore how NLP distinctions apply to their goals.

用户可请它帮助拆解他们自身的过程、建模他人的策略、创建逐步的练习，或探索 NLP 的区分如何应用于他们的目标。

其他机器人迪尔茨 AI 智能体Other Robot Dilts AI Agents

Other Robot Dilts AI agents include:

其他机器人迪尔茨 AI 智能体包括：

Robot Dilts Sleight of Mouth Thought Virus Detector Analyzes statements and identifies any key language patterns used in the statement to look for "red flags" relating to potential limiting beliefs and thought viruses and suggests possible Sleight of Mouth responses to transform or reframe potentially limiting beliefs.

机器人迪尔茨 巧舌如簧思想病毒侦测器（Sleight of Mouth Thought Virus Detector）分析陈述，辨识陈述中使用的任何关键语言模式，以寻找与潜在限制性信念和思想病毒相关的"红旗"，并建议可能的巧舌如簧（Sleight of Mouth）回应，以转化或重构潜在的限制性信念。

机器人迪尔茨 创想工程策略教练（Imagineering Strategy Coach）一个引导用户走过迪士尼创想工程策略——梦想家（Dreamer）、现实主义者（Realist）与批评者（Critic）——各阶段、并提出澄清性问题与建议的 AI 创造力教练与导师。

Robot Dilts Logical Leveler An AI assistant that supports users to understand and apply Robert Dilts' NeuroLogical Levels of Change model to manage change in every area of their lives

机器人迪尔茨 逻辑层次师（Logical Leveler）一个支撑用户理解并应用罗伯特·迪尔茨的神经逻辑改变层次（NeuroLogical Levels of Change）模型、以管理其生活各层面改变的 AI 助手。

Robot Dilts Pyramid Practice An AI assistant that guides users through a series of exercises that helps them to recognize and respond to statements at different Levels of Learning and Change

机器人迪尔茨 金字塔练习（Pyramid Practice）一个引导用户走过一系列练习、帮助他们辨识并回应处于不同学习与改变层次之陈述的 AI 助手。

Robot Dilts Meta Modeler

机器人迪尔茨 元模型师（Meta Modeler）

An AI language coach that helps users learn how to identify and clarify deletions, distortions and generalizations in their verbal representations of their outcomes, obstacles and the resources needed to reach their outcomes.

一个帮助用户学习如何辨识并澄清其关于结果、障碍与达成结果所需资源之言语表征中的删减、扭曲与概括的 AI 语言教练。

Robot Dilts Meta Model Mastery An AI language coach that helps users learn how to identify and clarify deletions, distortions and generalizations in their verbal representations of outcomes and obstacles.

机器人迪尔茨 元模型精通（Meta Model Mastery）一个帮助用户学习如何辨识并澄清其关于结果与障碍之言语表征中的删减、扭曲与概括的 AI 语言教练。

Robot Dilts Representational Systems Assistant An AI agent that helps users to practice identifying and responding to statements containing references to particular sensory representational systems and submodalities

机器人迪尔茨 表征系统助手（Representational Systems Assistant）一个帮助用户练习辨识并回应包含特定感官表征系统与次模态（submodalities）之陈述的 AI 智能体。

Robot Dilts COACH State Coach Helps users to develop, maintain and recover a robust COACH state: Centered, Open, Aware, Connected and Holding their experience from a curious, creative and resourceful state

机器人迪尔茨 COACH 状态教练（COACH State Coach）帮助用户发展、维持并恢复稳健的 COACH 状态：居中（Centered）、开放（Open）、觉察（Aware）、联结（Connected），并从一个好奇、创造、有资源的 state 涵容（Holding）其经验。

Robot Dilts Meta Programmer Analyzes user's statements to identify the most dominant meta program patterns and then helps to bring balance by asking questions that elicit complementary meta program attributes

机器人迪尔茨 元程序师（Meta Programmer）分析用户的陈述以辨识最主导的元程序（meta program）模式，然后透过提出能引出互补元程序属性的问题，帮助达成平衡。

Robot Dilts Meta Program Mastery An AI that helps users learn how to identify key meta program patterns in verbal representations of outcomes and obstacles.

机器人迪尔茨 元程序精通（Meta Program Mastery）一个帮助用户学习如何辨识结果与障碍之言语表征中关键元程序模式的 AI。

Success Factor Modeling Mentor Helps people apply the principles and models of Success Factor Modeling

成功要素建模导师（Success Factor Modeling Mentor）帮助人们应用成功要素建模的原则与模型。

Next Generation Entrepreneur AI Coach An AI assistant that helps entrepreneurs to build a robust Circle of Success

下一代创业家 AI 教练（Next Generation Entrepreneur AI Coach）一个帮助用户建立稳健成功圈（Circle of Success）的 AI 助手。

Next Generation Entrepreneurship Interview Guide (SFM-Based Agent) Guides the user through a structured entrepreneurial interview process, helping them articulate their experience, insights, and success patterns.

下一代创业访谈指南（SFM 本位智能体）〔Next Generation Entrepreneurship Interview Guide (SFM-Based Agent)〕引导用户走过结构化的创业访谈过程，帮助他们阐明其经验、洞见与成功模式。

[^1]: 贝特森初遇艾瑞克森是在一九三〇年代，当时他与时任妻子、著名人类学家玛格丽特·米德（Margaret Mead）正在巴厘岛研究 trance 舞蹈。他们有意获取关于催眠与 trance 的背景资料。贝特森与米德是通过一位共同的朋友、著名作家奥尔德斯·赫胥黎（Aldous Huxley，《美丽新世界》与《感知之门》的作者）介绍而结识艾瑞克森的。

[^1]: 据贝特森，问题不在于一个人是否精神分裂。他相信我们在很大程度上都是精神分裂的。对他而言，关键的区分在于一个人是 96% 精神分裂还是 98% 精神分裂，以及那差异在我们周遭更大系统中是如何被回应与容纳的。
